--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 4634
entity_slug: jesse-s-on-the-bay
google_review_name: places/ChIJ4xPax9AZmogRDXrwA4ucyqQ/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT21ST1IySjRNbkJsTVV0V1h6bE1lSGR6TjNkNldWRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: Jesse's on the Bay was an AMAZING upscale dining find while staying in Gulf Shores. We dined here on a Wednesday evening and came back the following Friday to have dinner here again.

We started both nights by having Manhattan's and appetizers at the bar. Seth was our bartender and he was wonderful and friendly. The crab cake was phenomenal and full of flavor! I loved the cilantro and the texture of the crab cake. Also, I've never had grits before and Seth gave us a sample of the grits with Gouda and I couldn't believe how good they were!

For dinner both nights, I ordered the 8 oz. filet. The first night I dined at Jesse's, I ordered the Jesse's steak sauce, which had a flavor profile of Worcestershire and sugary sweet. The second night, I ordered the Bernaise sauce. I was disappointed the Bernaise was put on top (and A LOT of it) because it took away from the flavor of the steak. Next time I know to request the sauce on the side.

The Allen Brothers steaks are amazing, the mood was a cross between casual and classy, the waterfront view was stunning, and the customer service was exceptional.

Only complaint is the second night we dined there, we waited over an hour for our food and it seems it was forgotten about. My steak was somewhat cold but it still tasted fantastic, so I didn't really care.

A 5/5 restaurant in my book and I would love to go back again!

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: 61bfc2d0-a45a-41e1-ae67-d4a2cf56170e
entity_slug: jesse-s-on-the-bay
reviewer_name: Meesh
rating: 5
body: Jesse's on the Bay was an AMAZING upscale dining find while staying in Gulf Shores. We dined here on a Wednesday evening and came back the following Friday to have dinner here again.

We started both nights by having Manhattan's and appetizers at the bar. Seth was our bartender and he was wonderful and friendly. The crab cake was phenomenal and full of flavor! I loved the cilantro and the texture of the crab cake. Also, I've never had grits before and Seth gave us a sample of the grits with Gouda and I couldn't believe how good they were!

For dinner both nights, I ordered the 8 oz. filet. The first night I dined at Jesse's, I ordered the Jesse's steak sauce, which had a flavor profile of Worcestershire and sugary sweet. The second night, I ordered the Bernaise sauce. I was disappointed the Bernaise was put on top (and A LOT of it) because it took away from the flavor of the steak. Next time I know to request the sauce on the side.

The Allen Brothers steaks are amazing, the mood was a cross between casual and classy, the waterfront view was stunning, and the customer service was exceptional.

Only complaint is the second night we dined there, we waited over an hour for our food and it seems it was forgotten about. My steak was somewhat cold but it still tasted fantastic, so I didn't really care.

A 5/5 restaurant in my book and I would love to go back again!

body: We had been to Jesse's in Magnolia Springs and were anxious to try this one in Fort Morgan. It has a glorious view of the bay which you can see from most seats. I had Shrimp & Grits, which was one of the best I've tried --  gouda grits and not too spicy. My husband had a huge porkchop which he thought was very good. A bit on the pricey side but we'll worth it. Pleasant atmosphere and attentive waitstaff..

Overall, it was a wonderful evening, and I can’t wait to come back for more!

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: d5b47243-de48-404c-b43c-2426e9056ed5
entity_slug: jesse-s-on-the-bay
reviewer_name: Michael Cummings
rating: 5
body: *Exceptional Dining Experience*

I am compelled to express my utmost admiration for the extraordinary service and culinary excellence experienced at your esteemed establishment. From the moment we were greeted with warmth and professionalism, to the gracious farewell extended by Manager Derick, every aspect of our visit was meticulously crafted to exceed expectations.

The breathtaking sunset vista over the water created an idyllic ambiance, perfectly complemented by the impeccable service provided by our server, Mia. Her expertise and attentiveness were truly exemplary, elevating our dining experience to unparalleled heights.

A special commendation is due to the Executive Chef, whose flexibility and dedication to culinary innovation accommodated our unique request with ease. The pièce de résistance was, without a doubt, the masterfully prepared filet and pork chop, which must be sampled by anyone seeking to indulge in true epicurean delight.

The dessert course, featuring the sublime cranberry cheesecake adorned with shaved white chocolate, was a masterclass in texture and flavor harmony. The extensive wine and liquor selection further underscored the restaurant's commitment to providing an unparalleled gastronomic journey.

In conclusion, I wholeheartedly affirm that every aspect of our experience was nothing short of extraordinary, justifying the premium pricing and solidifying my conviction that this restaurant is an absolute gem. I eagerly anticipate returning to savor more of the exceptional cuisine and service that defines your establishment.
